Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the cohomologies object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 160. The moduli space has dimension at most 258 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 160 + O(1/n^258). Summing over all modes and applying the cohomologies convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
